###

ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ruler_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = {
			trigger = {
				is_ai = yes
				faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
				NOT = { faith = { has_doctrine = muhammad_succession_berberist_doctrine } }
				has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_active_yes
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				current_date >= 950.1.1
				has_any_nickname = no
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = no } # Caliphs have their own titles to take
				OR = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_feudal
					government_has_flag = government_is_clan
					government_has_flag = government_is_administrative
				}
			}
			modifier = { 
				add = 900
				OR = {
					scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_din_nickname_trigger = yes }
					scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_islam_nickname_trigger = yes }
					scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_dawlah_nickname_trigger = yes }
					scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_mulk_nickname_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # A small possibility for Andalusians and Levantines to take up an honorific with the 'Dawlah' ending
				add = 10
				current_date >= 950.1.1
				current_date < 1070.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_europe_west_iberia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					culture = culture:andalusian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:andalusian } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # A small possibility for Andalusians to take up an honorific with the 'Dawlah' ending (Post 1070)
				add = 10
				current_date >= 1070.1.1
				current_date < 1130.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_europe_west_iberia }
					culture = culture:andalusian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:andalusian } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # A small possibility for Muslims in the Mashriq and Hijaz to take up an honorific with the 'Din' ending (Post 1070)
				add = 10
				current_date >= 1070.1.1
				current_date < 1100.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_amman } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_jawf } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_medina } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_mecca } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # (35.7%) A significant possibility for Muslims in the Middle east post 1100 to take up a honorific
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1100.1.1
				current_date < 1250.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_persia }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_amman } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_jawf } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_medina } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_mecca } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
					culture = culture:turkmen
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:turkmen } }
					culture = culture:persian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:persian } }
					culture = culture:kurdish
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:kurdish } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # (35.7%) A significant possibility for Muslims anywhere post 1250 to take up a honorific
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1250.1.1
			}
			
			ME_islamic_honorific_ai_start_effect = yes
		}
		90 = {
			# Nothing...
		}
	}
}

ME_islamic_honorific_ai_start_effect = {
	random_list = {
		1 = {
			modifier = { # -Din
				add = 900
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_din_nickname_trigger = yes }
			}
			modifier = { # -Din ending honorifics become popular in the Mashriq post 1070 (Sees some use in Hejaz)
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1070.1.1
				current_date < 1100.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_amman } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_jawf } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_medina } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_mecca } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # -Din ending honorifics become popular in the Mashriq and Persianate world post 1070
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1100.1.1
				current_date < 1250.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_persia }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_amman } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_jawf } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_medina } }
					capital_province = { county = { duchy = title:d_mecca } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
					culture = culture:turkmen
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:turkmen } }
					culture = culture:persian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:persian } }
					culture = culture:kurdish
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:kurdish } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # Honorifics with the -Din ending becoming very  popular post 1250
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1250.1.1
			}
			
			ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ud_din_effect = yes
		}
		1 = {
			modifier = { # -Islam
				add = 900
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_islam_nickname_trigger = yes }
			}
			
			ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ul_islam_effect = yes
		}
		1 = {
			modifier = { # -Dawlah
				add = 900
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_dawlah_nickname_trigger = yes }
			}
			modifier = { # 950-1070, Andalusians and Levantines taking up honorifics with the 'Dawlah' ending
				add = 50
				current_date >= 950.1.1
				current_date < 1070.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_europe_west_iberia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_middle_east_jerusalem }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_mesopotamia }
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_jazira }
					culture = culture:andalusian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:andalusian } }
					culture = culture:levantine
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:levantine } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { # 1070-1130 Andalusians taking up honorifics with the 'Dawlah' ending
				add = 50
				current_date >= 1070.1.1
				current_date < 1130.1.1
				OR = {
					capital_province = { geographical_region = world_europe_west_iberia }
					culture = culture:andalusian
					culture = { any_parent_culture_or_above = { this = culture:andalusian } }
				}
			}
			
			ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ud_dawlah_effect = yes
		}
		1 = {
			modifier = { # -Mulk
				add = 900
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_mulk_nickname_trigger = yes }
			}
			
			ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ul_mulk_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

ME_caliphal_honorific_ai_ruler_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			has_title = title:d_sunni
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_sunni_historic_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			has_title = title:d_muwalladi
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_muwalladi_historic_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			has_title = title:d_masmudi
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_masmudi_historic_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			OR = {
				has_title = title:d_shiite
				has_title = title:d_hafizi
			}
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_ismaili_historic_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			has_title = title:d_nizari
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_nizari_historic_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
			}
			has_title = title:d_zayidi
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_zayidi_historic_effect = yes
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_title = title:d_sunni
				has_title = title:d_muwalladi
				has_title = title:d_masmudi
				has_title = title:d_shiite
				has_title = title:d_hafizi
				faith = { has_doctrine = muhammad_succession_sunni_doctrine }
				ME_has_caliphal_short_nickname_trigger = yes
				ME_has_caliphal_long_nickname_trigger = yes
			}
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_caliphal_random_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			#OR = {
			#	has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_long
			#	has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
			#}
			OR = {
				has_title = title:d_zayidi
				faith = faith:zayidi
				faith = faith:salihi
			}
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_caliphal_honorific_zayidi_random_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_din_nickname_trigger = yes }
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_islam_nickname_trigger = yes }
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ud_dawlah_nickname_trigger = yes }
				scope:previous_holder = { ME_has_ul_mulk_nickname_trigger = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_active_no }
			has_any_nickname = no
		}
		ME_islamic_honorific_ai_start_effect = yes
	}
}

ME_islamic_honorific_ai_ud_din_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = {
			trigger = {

				}
				is_male = yes
				culture = { has_cultural_pillar = language_arabic }
			}
			modifier = { 
				add = 50
				learning >= 10

ME_caliphal_honorific_sunni_historic_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_sunni_var } }
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 867.1.1 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
				value = 16
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1066.9.15 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
				value = 27
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1178.10.1 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
				value = 34
			}
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_sunni_var = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
				}
			}
			give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
				}
			}
			give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur_nonprefix
		}
		
		change_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			add = 1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_sunni_var = 3
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_mahdi
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_mahdi_nonprefix
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_mahdi_billah
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}

ME_caliphal_honorific_muwalladi_historic_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var } }
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1066.9.15 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
				value = 13
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1178.10.1 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
				value = 13
			}
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir_nonprefix
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir_li_din_allah
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir_li_din_allah_nonprefix
			}
		}
		
		change_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
			add = 1

ME_caliphal_honorific_masmudi_historic_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var } }
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1178.10.1 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
				value = 3
			}
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var < 3
		}
		change_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			add = 1
		}
	}
	else_if = {

			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var = 3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
				}
			}
			give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
					has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
				}
			}
			give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur_nonprefix
		}
		
		change_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			add = 1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var = 4
		}

			give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir_nonprefix

			name = caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			add = 1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_masmudi_var = 5

ME_caliphal_honorific_ismaili_historic_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_ismaili_var } }
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1066.9.15 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_ismaili_var
				value = 9
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date = 1178.10.1 }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = caliph_laqab_ismaili_var
				value = 15
			}
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { 
			exists = global_var:caliph_laqab_ismaili_var
			global_var:caliph_laqab_ismaili_var = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_qaim
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				give_nickname = nick_me_al_qaim_nonprefix
			}

ME_caliphal_honorific_startup = {
	## Laqab Variables
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date = 867.1.1 }
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			value = 16
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { game_start_date = 1066.9.15 }
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			value = 27
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
			value = 13
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_ismaili_var
			value = 9
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_zayidi_var
			value = 11
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { game_start_date = 1178.10.1 }
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_sunni_var
			value = 34
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_muwalladi_var
			value = 13
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_masmudi_var
			value = 3
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_ismaili_var
			value = 15
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_nizari_var
			value = 7
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = caliph_laqab_zayidi_var
			value = 12
		}
	}

	## Abbasid Caliphs
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 754.6.10 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:34002 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:34002 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mansur_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_no }
			character:34002 = {
				remove_nickname = yes
				change_first_name = Al-Mansur
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 775.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:34003 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mahdi }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:34003 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mahdi_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_no }
			character:34003 = {
				remove_nickname = yes
				change_first_name = Al-Mahdi
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 785.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:34004 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_hadi }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:34004 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_hadi_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_no }
			character:34004 = {
				remove_nickname = yes
				change_first_name = Al-Hadi
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 809.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:34006 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_amin }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:34006 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_amin_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_no }
			character:34006 = {
				remove_nickname = yes
				change_first_name = Al-Amin
			}
		}

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_4 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_hadi }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_4 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_hadi_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_long
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_4 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_hadi_billah_nonprefix }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 1136.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_3 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_muhtadi }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_3 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_muhtadi_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_long
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_3 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_muhtadi_billah_nonprefix }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { game_start_date >= 1158.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_2 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_qahir }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular

				}

			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_long
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:nizarid_2 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_qahir_biquwa_allah_nonprefix }
			}
		}
	}
	
	## Random Claimants
	if = { # Sijilmasa's 'ash-Shakir Lillah'
		limit = { game_start_date >= 945.1.1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_short
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_short
				}

					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_both
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_caliphal
					}
				}
				character:me_sijilmasa_011 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_shakir }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:me_sijilmasa_011 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_shakir_nonprefix }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					#has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_random_long
					has_game_rule = me_caliphal_honorific_active_historic_long
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_no
						has_game_rule = me_islamic_honorific_prefixed_regular
					}
				}
				character:me_sijilmasa_011 = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_shakir_lillah_nonprefix }

		}
	}
